    index0 = 64*p0 + p1
    index1 = p1
    def body(self, ops):

        load = ops.load('buf26', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf28', get_index_1)
        sub = ops.sub(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf29', get_index_2)
        constant = ops.constant(128.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_2, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('primals_12', get_index_3)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, load_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_4 = ops.load('primals_13', get_index_4)
        add_1 = ops.add(mul_1, load_4)
        relu = ops.relu(add_1)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf31', get_index_5, relu, None)
        return store

    index0 = 256*p0 + p1
    index1 = p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf79', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf81', get_index_1)
        sub = ops.sub(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf82', get_index_2)
        constant = ops.constant(8.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_2, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('primals_66', get_index_3)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, load_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_4 = ops.load('primals_67', get_index_4)
        add_1 = ops.add(mul_1, load_4)
        relu = ops.relu(add_1)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf83', get_index_5, relu, None)
        return store

class op105_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 2, p1: 512}
    index0 = 512*p0 + p1
    index1 = p1
    index2 = p1 + 512
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf103', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf104', get_index_1)
        sub = ops.sub(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf103', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf104', get_index_3)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(load_2, load_3)
        square = ops.square(sub_1)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf103', get_index_4)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_5 = ops.load('buf104', get_index_5)
        sub_2 = ops.sub(load_4, load_5)
        square_1 = ops.square(sub_2)
        add = ops.add(square, square_1)
        constant = ops.constant(2.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(add, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_6 = ops.load('primals_96', get_index_6)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, load_6)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_7 = ops.load('primals_97', get_index_7)
        add_2 = ops.add(mul_1, load_7)
        relu = ops.relu(add_2)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf105', get_index_8, relu, None)
        return store

class op110_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 2, p1: 512}
    index0 = 512*p0 + p1
    index1 = p1
    index2 = p1 + 512
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf106', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf107', get_index_1)
        sub = ops.sub(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf106', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf107', get_index_3)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(load_2, load_3)
        square = ops.square(sub_1)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf106', get_index_4)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_5 = ops.load('buf107', get_index_5)
        sub_2 = ops.sub(load_4, load_5)
        square_1 = ops.square(sub_2)
        add = ops.add(square, square_1)
        constant = ops.constant(2.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(add, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_6 = ops.load('primals_102', get_index_6)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, load_6)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_7 = ops.load('primals_103', get_index_7)
        add_2 = ops.add(mul_1, load_7)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_8 = ops.load('buf108', get_index_8)
        get_index_9 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_9 = ops.load('buf109', get_index_9)
        sub_3 = ops.sub(load_8, load_9)
        get_index_10 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_10 = ops.load('buf108', get_index_10)
        get_index_11 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_11 = ops.load('buf109', get_index_11)
        sub_4 = ops.sub(load_10, load_11)
        square_2 = ops.square(sub_4)
        get_index_12 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_12 = ops.load('buf108', get_index_12)
        get_index_13 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_13 = ops.load('buf109', get_index_13)
        sub_5 = ops.sub(load_12, load_13)
        square_3 = ops.square(sub_5)
        add_3 = ops.add(square_2, square_3)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(2.0, torch.float32)
        truediv_1 = ops.truediv(add_3, constant_2)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_4 = ops.add(truediv_1, constant_3)
        rsqrt_1 = ops.rsqrt(add_4)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sub_3, rsqrt_1)
        get_index_14 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_14 = ops.load('primals_108', get_index_14)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(mul_2, load_14)
        get_index_15 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_15 = ops.load('primals_109', get_index_15)
        add_5 = ops.add(mul_3, load_15)
        add_6 = ops.add(add_2, add_5)
        relu = ops.relu(add_6)
        get_index_16 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf110', get_index_16, relu, None)
        return store

class op113_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 2, p1: 512}
    index0 = 512*p0 + p1
    index1 = p1
    index2 = p1 + 512
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf111', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf112', get_index_1)
        sub = ops.sub(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf111', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf112', get_index_3)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(load_2, load_3)
        square = ops.square(sub_1)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf111', get_index_4)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_5 = ops.load('buf112', get_index_5)
        sub_2 = ops.sub(load_4, load_5)
        square_1 = ops.square(sub_2)
        add = ops.add(square, square_1)
        constant = ops.constant(2.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(add, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_6 = ops.load('primals_114', get_index_6)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, load_6)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_7 = ops.load('primals_115', get_index_7)
        add_2 = ops.add(mul_1, load_7)
        relu = ops.relu(add_2)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf113', get_index_8, relu, None)
        return store

        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)

        return store
